and commercialization to empower its growth. We will             三角、大灣區區域的發展、同時亦兼顧重點
focus on the business development in the Beijing-Tianjin-        城市群。公司將聚焦優勢專科、明確專科連
Hebei Region, the Yangtze River Delta and the Greater Bay        鎖集群轉型的發展定位，成為具備良好臨床

assistance to other medical institutes in Wuhan City             密切接觸者訪視、疑似患者轉運等工作。而
including Wuhan Jinyintan Hospital and certain mobile cabin      華潤醫療分佈在北京、江蘇、山東、安徽、

has also immediately established an emergency supply team
in launching an emergency procurement plan, contacting

prevention and control materials to our member institutes in
different regions in the situation of tight supply. During the
COVID-19 epidemic, CR Medical made effective use of the
three-dimensional anti-epidemic model of “Wuhan frontline
+ designated hospitals in various regions + fever clinics &
community prevention + promising group supply chain”,
taking an importing role and taking great responsibility in
virus fighting as a social capital medical group.

     Regarding our resumption of normal operation and business        就復工安排和業務恢復方面，成員醫療機構
     recovery, our member medical institutes have implemented         實 施 了 分 類、分 級 精 準 復 工 生 產。疫 情 期
     graded and accurate resumption of production. During the         間，成 員 醫 院 管 理 團 隊 及 全 體 員 工 放 棄 休
     epidemic, the member hospital management team and all            假，全部在崗在位。華潤醫療根據所屬各醫

     operation. The Company intends to share its profits with

Opinion                                                         意見
We have audited the consolidated financial statements of        我們已審計列載於第101至259頁的華潤醫療
China Resources Medical Holdings Company Limited (the           控股有限公司（「貴公司」）及其附屬公司（「貴
“Company”) and its subsidiaries (the “Group”) set out on        集團」）的綜合財務報表，此綜合財務報表包
pages 101 to 259, which comprise the consolidated               括於二零二零年十二月三十一日的綜合財務
statement of financial position as at 31 December 2020, and     狀況報表與截至該日止年度的綜合損益報
the consolidated statement of profit or loss, the               表、綜合全面收益報表、綜合權益變動報表
consolidated statement of comprehensive income, the             和綜合現金流量報表，以及綜合財務報表附
consolidated statement of changes in equity and the             註，包括主要會計政策概要。
consolidated statement of cash flows for the year then
ended, and notes to the consolidated financial statements,
including a summary of significant accounting policies.

In our opinion, the consolidated financial statements give a    我們認為，該等綜合財務報表已根據國際會
true and fair view of the consolidated financial position of    計準則理事會頒布的《國際財務報告準則》真
the Group as at 31 December 2020, and of its consolidated       實而中肯地反映了 貴集團於二零二零年
financial performance and its consolidated cash flows for the   十二月三十一日的綜合財務狀況及截至該日
year then ended in accordance with International Financial      止年度的綜合財務表現及綜合現金流量，並
Reporting Standards (“IFRSs”) issued by the International       已 遵 照 香 港《公 司 條 例》的 披 露 規 定 妥 為 擬
Accounting Standards Board (“IASB”) and have been               備。
properly prepared in compliance with the disclosure

     Basis for opinion                                                意見的基礎
     We conducted our audit in accordance with Hong Kong              我們已根據香港會計師公會頒布的《香港審
     Standards on Auditing (“HKSAs”) issued by the Hong Kong          計準則》進行審計。我們在該等準則下承擔
     Institute of Certified Public Accountants (“HKICPA”). Our        的責任已在本報告「核數師就審計綜合財務
     responsibilities under those standards are further described     報表承擔的責任 」部分中作進一步闡述。根
     in the Auditor’s responsibilities for the audit of the           據香港會計師公會頒布的《專業會計師道德
     consolidated financial statements section of our report. We      守則》
                                                                        （以下簡稱「守則」），我們獨立於 貴集
     are independent of the Group in accordance with the              團，並已履行守則中的其他專業道德責任。
     HKICPA’s Code of Ethics for Professional Accountants (the        我們相信，我們所獲得的審計憑證能充足及
     “Code”), and we have fulfilled our other ethical                 適當地為我們的審計意見提供基礎。

          AND DISCLOSURES (continued)
           (b)   Amendments to IFRS 3 clarify and provide                   (b)   國際財務報告準則第3號之修訂澄
                 additional guidance on the definition of a                       清業務的定義及就此提供額外指
                 business. The amendments clarify that for an                     引。有關修訂澄清，倘一套活動
                 integrated set of activities and assets to be                    及資產被視為業務，最少必需包
                 considered a business, it must include, at a                     括共同大幅貢獻產生輸出物的能
                 minimum, an input and a substantive process that                 力的一項輸入項目及一個實質程
                 together significantly contribute to the ability to              序。業務毋須擁有所有產生輸出
                 create output. A business can exist without                      物所需的輸入項目及程序而存
                 including all of the inputs and processes needed                 在。有關修訂取消就市場參與者
                 to create outputs. The amendments remove the                     能否收購業務及持續生產輸出物
                 assessment of whether market participants are                    作出評估。取而代之，重點乃所
                 capable of acquiring the business and continue to                取得的輸入項目及所取得的實質
                 produce outputs. Instead, the focus is on                        程序能否共同大幅貢獻產生輸出
                 whether acquired inputs and acquired substantive                 物的能力。有關修訂亦收窄輸出
                 processes together significantly contribute to the               物的定義，專注於向客戶提供的
                 ability to create outputs. The amendments have                   貨物或服務、投資收入或來自日
                 also narrowed the definition of outputs to focus                 常活動的其他收入。此外，有關
                 on goods or services provided to customers,                      修訂就所取得的程序是否實質作
                 investment income or other income from ordinary                  出評估提供指引，並引入可選擇
                 activities. Furthermore, the amendments provide                  的公允價值集中測試，以允許就
                 guidance to assess whether an acquired process                   所取得的一套活動及資產是否並
                 is substantive and introduce an optional fair value              非業務作出簡化評估。本集團已
                 concentration test to permit a simplified                        對2020年1月1日或之後發生的交
                 assessment of whether an acquired set of                         易或其他事件，按未來基準應用
                 activities and assets is not a business. The Group               有關修訂。有關修訂並無對本集
                 has applied the amendments prospectively to                      團的財務狀況和表現造成任何影
                 transactions or other events that occurred on or                 響。
                 after 1 January 2020. The amendments did not
                 have any impact on the financial position and
                 performance of the Group.

           (c)   Amendments to IFRS 9, IAS 39 and IFRS 7                    (c)   國際財務報告準則第9號、國際會
                 address issues affecting financial reporting in the              計準則第39號及國際財務報告準
                 period before the replacement of an existing                     則第7號之修訂應對在以替代無風
                 interest rate benchmark with an alternative risk-                險利率（「無風險利率」）取代現有
                 free rate (“RFR”). The amendments provide                        利率基準之前期間影響財務報告
                 temporary reliefs which enable hedge accounting                  的問題。有關修訂提供了暫時性
                 to continue during the period of uncertainty                     的救濟，使對沖會計能夠在推出
                 before the introduction of the alternative RFR. In               替代無風險利率前的不確定時期
                 addition, the amendments require companies to                    內繼續進行。此外，有關修訂要
                 provide additional information to investors about                求公司向投資者提供有關直接受
                 their hedging relationships which are directly                   這些不確定因素影響的對沖關係
                 affected by these uncertainties. The amendments                  的更多資料。由於本集團並無任
                 did not have any impact on the financial position                何利率對沖關係，故有關修訂並
                 and performance of the Group as the Group does                   無對本集團的財務狀況和表現造
                 not have any interest rate hedging relationships.                成任何影響。

           beginning of the earliest period presented in the                不會對本集團的財務報表造成任何重大

           amendments. Earlier application is permitted. The

2.4 SUMMARY OF SIGNIFICANT                                    2.4 主要會計政策概要
    ACCOUNTING POLICIES
  Investments in associates and joint                           於聯營公司及合營公司之投資
  ventures
  An associate is an entity in which the Group has a            聯營公司為本集團於其一般不少於20%
  long term interest of generally not less than 20% of          股本投票權中擁有長期權益的實體，且
  the equity voting rights and over which it is in a            可對其發揮重大影響力。重大影響力指
  position to exercise significant influence. Significant       的是參與投資對象的財務和經營決策的
  influence is the power to participate in the financial        權力，但不是控制或共同控制這些決策
  and operating policy decisions of the investee, but is        的權力。
  not control or joint control over those policies.

  A joint venture is a type of joint arrangement whereby        合營公司指一類共同安排，對安排擁有
  the parties that have joint control of the arrangement        共同控制權之訂約方據此有權享有共同
  have rights to the net assets of the joint venture. Joint     安排之資產淨值。共同控制指對某項安
  control is the contractually agreed sharing of control of     排之訂約協定共同控制，共同控制僅於
  an arrangement, which exists only when decisions              相關活動之決定需共享控制權之各方一
  about the relevant activities require the unanimous           致同意之時存在。
  consent of the parties sharing control.

  The Group’s investments in associates and a joint             本集團於聯營公司及合營公司的投資乃
  venture are stated in the consolidated statement of           按本集團根據權益會計法應佔資產淨值
  financial position at the Group’s share of net assets         減任何減值虧損於綜合財務狀況報表列
  under the equity method of accounting, less any               賬。倘會計政策存在任何不一致，將會
  impairment losses. Adjustments are made to bring into         作出相應調整。本集團應佔聯營公司及
  line any dissimilar accounting policies that may exist.       合營公司收購後業績及其他全面收入分
  The Group’s share of the post-acquisition results and         別計入綜合損益報表及綜合其他全面收
  other comprehensive income of associates and a joint          入。此外，倘於聯營公司或合營公司的
  venture is included in the consolidated statement of          權益直接確認變動，則本集團會於綜合
  profit or loss and consolidated other comprehensive           權益變動報表確認其應佔任何變動（倘
  income, respectively. In addition, when there has been        適用）。本集團與其聯營公司或合營公
  a change recognised directly in the equity of the             司間交易的未變現收益及虧損將以本集
  associate or joint venture, the Group recognises its          團於聯營公司或合營公司的投資為限對
  share of any changes, when applicable, in the                 銷，惟倘未變現虧損證明所轉讓資產減
  consolidated statement of changes in equity.                  值則除外。收購聯營公司或合營公司所
  Unrealised gains and losses resulting from transactions       產生的商譽已計入作本集團於聯營公司
  between the Group and its associates or joint venture         或合營公司投資的一部分。
  are eliminated to the extent of the Group’s
  investments in the associates or joint venture, except
  where unrealised losses provide evidence of an
  impairment of the assets transferred. Goodwill arising

  associates or joint venture.

2.4 SUMMARY OF SIGNIFICANT                                      2.4 主要會計政策概要（續）
    ACCOUNTING POLICIES (continued)
   Fair value measurement                                         公允價值計量
   The Group measures its financial assets at FVTPL at            本集團於各報告期末按公允價值計量以
   fair value at the end of each reporting period. Fair           公允價值計量且其變動計入損益的金融
   value is the price that would be received to sell an           資產。公允價值乃在市場參與者於計量
   asset or paid to transfer a liability in an orderly            日期進行的有序交易中出售資產所收取
   transaction between market participants at the                 或轉移負債所支付的價格。公允價值計
   measurement date. The fair value measurement is                量乃基於假設出售資產或轉移負債的交
   based on the presumption that the transaction to sell          易於資產或負債的主要市場進行或於未
   the asset or transfer the liability takes place either in      有主要市場的情況下，則於資產或負債
   the principal market for the asset or liability, or in the     的最有利市場進行。主要或最有利市場
   absence of a principal market, in the most                     須位於本集團能到達的地方。資產或負
   advantageous market for the asset or liability. The            債的公允價值乃基於市場參與者為資產
   principal or the most advantageous market must be              或負債定價所用的假設計量（假設市場
   accessible by the Group. The fair value of an asset or         參與者依照彼等的最佳經濟利益行事）
                                                                                  。
   a liability is measured using the assumptions that
   market participants would use when pricing the asset
   or liability, assuming that market participants act in
   their economic best interest.

   The Group uses valuation techniques that are                   本集團使用適用於不同情況的估值方
   appropriate in the circumstances and for which                 法，而其有足夠資料計量公允價值，以
   sufficient data are available to measure fair value,           盡量使用相關可觀察輸入數據及盡量減
   maximising the use of relevant observable inputs and           少使用不可觀察輸入數據。
   minimising the use of unobservable inputs.

   All assets and liabilities for which fair value is             公允價值於財務報表計量或披露的所有
   measured or disclosed in the financial statements are          資產及負債基於對公允價值計量整體屬
   categorised within the fair value hierarchy, described         重要的最低水平輸入數據按如下所述在
   as follows, based on the lowest level input that is            公允價值層級中分類：
   significant to the fair value measurement as a whole:

   Level 1 — based on quoted prices (unadjusted) in               第一級   — 根據可識別資產或負債於
             active markets for identical assets or                      活躍市場中所報未調整價
             liabilities                                                 格

   Level 2 — based on valuation techniques for which              第二級   — 根據對公允價值計量具有
             the lowest level input that is significant to               重大影響的最低水平輸入
             the fair value measurement is observable,                   數據可直接或間接被觀察
             either directly or indirectly                               的估值方法

   Level 3 — based on valuation techniques for which              第三級   — 根據對公允價值計量有重
             the lowest level input that is significant to               大影響的最低水平輸入數
             the fair value measurement is                               據不可觀察的估值方法

           (other than inventories, contract assets, deferred tax           遞延稅項資產及金融資產除外），便會
           assets and financial assets), the asset’s recoverable            估計資產的可收回金額。資產的可收回
           amount is estimated. An asset’s recoverable amount is            金額按資產或現金產生單位的使用價值
           the higher of the asset’s or cash-generating unit’s              及公允價值（以較高者為準）減出售成

           and is determined for an individual asset, unless the            有關資產並不產生現金流入，且在頗大

           assets, in which case the recoverable amount is                  回金額。
           determined for the cash-generating unit to which the
           asset belongs.

           An impairment loss is recognised only if the carrying            減值虧損僅於資產賬面值超過其可收回
           amount of an asset exceeds its recoverable amount. In            金額時予以確認。於評估使用價值時，

           assessments of the time value of money and the risks             其產生期內在損益表內扣除。
           specific to the asset. An impairment loss is charged to

   Leases                                                         租賃
   The Group assesses at contract inception whether a             本集團於合約初始評估有關合約是否屬

   contains, a lease if the contract conveys the right to         授予在一段期間內已識別資產之使用控

   time in exchange for consideration.

           Impairment of financial assets                                    金融資產減值
           The Group recognises an allowance for expected credit             本集團確認對並非以公允價值計量且其
           losses (“ECLs”) for all debt instruments not held at fair         變動計入損益的所有債務工具預期信貸
           value through profit or loss. ECLs are based on the               虧 損（「預 期 信 貸 虧 損」）的 撥 備。預 期
           difference between the contractual cash flows due in              信貸虧損乃基於根據合約到期的合約現
           accordance with the contract and all the cash flows               金流量與本集團預期收取的所有現金流
           that the Group expects to receive, discounted at an               量之間的差額而釐定，並以原實際利率
           approximation of the original effective interest rate. The        的近似值貼現。預期現金流量將包括出
           expected cash flows will include cash flows from the              售所持抵押的現金流量或組成合約條款
           sale of collateral held or other credit enhancements              的其他信貸提升措施。
           that are integral to the contractual terms.

           General approach                                                  一般方法
           ECLs are recognised in two stages. For credit                    預期信貸虧損分兩個階段確認。就自首
           exposures for which there has not been a significant             次確認起未有顯著增加的信貸風險而
           increase in credit risk since initial recognition, ECLs are      言，預 期 信 貸 虧 損 為 未 來12個 月 內 可
           provided for credit losses that result from default              能發生違約事件而導致的信貸虧損（12
           events that are possible within the next 12 months (a            個月預期信貸虧損）計提撥備。就自初
           12-month ECL). For those credit exposures for which              始確認起經已顯著增加的信貸風險而
           there has been a significant increase in credit risk             言，不論何時發生違約，於餘下風險年
           since initial recognition, a loss allowance is required for      期內的預期信貸虧損均須計提虧損撥備
           credit losses expected over the remaining life of the            （全期預期信貸虧損）。
           exposure, irrespective of the timing of the default (a
           lifetime ECL).

           At each reporting date, the Group assesses whether                於各報告日期，本集團評估金融工具的
           the credit risk on a financial instrument has increased           信貸風險自首次確認以來是否大幅增
           significantly since initial recognition. When making the          加。進行評估時，本集團會比較報告日
           assessment, the Group compares the risk of a default              期就金融工具產生的違約風險與於首次
           occurring on the financial instrument as at the                   確認日期就金融工具產生的違約風險，
           reporting date with the risk of a default occurring on            並會考慮合理及有理據且毋須花費過度
           the financial instrument as at the date of initial                成本或精力即可獲得的資料，包括過往
           recognition and considers reasonable and supportable              經驗及前瞻性資料。
           information that is available without undue cost or
           effort, including historical and forward-looking
           information.

   Impairment of financial assets (continued)                    金融資產減值（續）
   General approach (continued)                                  一般方法（續）
   The Group considers a financial asset in default when         倘 合 約 付 款 已 逾 期90日，則 本 集 團 認
   contractual payments are 90 days past due. However,           為 金 融 資 產 違 約。然 而，在 若 干 情 況
   in certain cases, the Group may also consider a               下，倘內部或外部資料顯示，在沒有計
   financial asset to be in default when internal or             及本集團所持有的任何信貸提升措施
   external information indicates that the Group is unlikely     前，本集團不大可能悉數收到未償還合
   to receive the outstanding contractual amounts in full        約款項，則本集團亦可認為金融資產違
   before taking into account any credit enhancements            約。倘 無 法 合 理 預 期 收 回 合 約 現 金 流
   held by the Group. A financial asset is written off           量，則撇銷金融資產。
   when there is no reasonable expectation of recovering
   the contractual cash flows.

   Financial assets at amortised cost are subject to             按攤銷成本計量的金融資產可按一般方
   impairment under the general approach and they are            法予以減值，並分類至下列不同階段以
   classified within the following stages for measurement        計量預期信貸虧損（不包括使用簡化方
   of ECLs except for trade receivables and contract             法之應收貿易款項及合約資產），詳情
   assets which apply the simplified approach as detailed        如下。
   below.

             has not increased significantly since initial              次確認以後並無顯著增
             recognition and for which the loss                         加，其 虧 損 撥 備 按 等 同

           experience, adjusted for forward-looking factors specific

   •    net interest expense or income                           •    淨利息開支或收入

   Dividends                                                     股息
   Final dividends are recognised as a liability when they       末期股息在股東大會上獲股東批准時確
   are approved by the shareholders in a general meeting.        認為負債。建議末期股息於財務報表附
   Proposed final dividends are disclosed in the notes to        註披露。
   the financial statements.

   Interim dividends are simultaneously proposed and             由於本公司的組織章程大綱及細則授予
   declared, because the Company’s memorandum and                董事宣派中期股息的權利，故本公司同

           Group to make estimates and assumptions that can                大影響之估計及假設。
           materially affect its consolidated financial statements.

           Any difference in the purchase price and the fair               所購入可識別淨資產之購買價與公允價
           values of the net identifiable assets acquired is               值之間的任何差額於綜合財務狀況報表
           recorded as either goodwill in the consolidated                 入賬列為商譽，或於損益表入賬列為議
           statement of financial position or gain on bargain              價收購收益。本集團進行之業務收購產
           purchase in profit or loss. The Group’s business                生商譽，而商譽須每年及於有跡象顯示
           acquisitions have resulted in goodwill, which is subject        商譽可能減值時檢測減值。本集團亦於
           to yearly impairment testing and whenever there is an           各報告期末評估所有非金融資產（包括

                 various treatment components that are all highly                            相關及被視為單一履約義務的治
                 interdependent and regarded as a single                                     療部分。住院時間通常是九天之
                 performance obligation. The period of in-patient                            內。由於病人同時收取及消耗本
                 services is usually within nine days. Since the                             集團進行醫務治療的利益，住院
                 patient simultaneously receives and consumes                                服務的相關收益在合約期間通過
                 the benefits of the Group’s performance in the                              參考完成履約義務的進度而被確
                 medical treatment, the relevant revenue of in-                              認。完成履約義務的進度乃透過
                 patient services is recognised over the period of                           直接計量本集團向客戶轉讓的個
                 the contract by reference to the progress towards                           別產品或服務的價值來衡量。
                 complete satisfaction of that performance
                 obligation. The progress towards the complete
                 satisfaction of performance obligation is

                 individual products or services transferred by the

      48. FAIR VALUE AND FAIR VALUE                                      48. 金融工具的公允價值及公允
          HIERARCHY OF FINANCIAL                                             價值層級
          INSTRUMENTS
           Management has assessed that the fair values of cash              管理層評估了現金及現金等價物、已抵
           and cash equivalents, pledged deposits, trade and bills           押存款、應收貿易款項及應收票據、計
           receivables, financial assets included in prepayments,            入預付款項、保證金及其他應收款項的
           deposits and other receivables, balances with related             金融資產、與關聯方的結餘、其他按攤
           parties, the current portion of other financial assets at         銷成本計量的金融資產的即期部分、應
           amortised cost, trade and bills payables, financial               付貿易款項及應付票據、計入其他應付
           liabilities included in other payables and accruals,              款項及應計費用的金融負債、應付舉辦
           payables to the Sponsored Hospitals and interest-                 權醫院款項以及計息銀行借款，由於這
           bearing bank borrowings approximate to their carrying             些工具到期期限較短，所以其公允價值
           amounts largely due to the short term maturities of               接近賬面值。
           these instruments.

           The fair values of the financial assets and liabilities are      金融資產及負債的公允價值按當前交易
           included at the amount at which the instrument could             （強制或清算出售除外）中雙方自願 交
           be exchanged in a current transaction between willing            換工具的金額入賬。以下方法和假設被
           parties, other than in a forced or liquidation sale. The         用於評估公允價值：
           following methods and assumptions were used to
           estimate the fair values:

           The fair values of receivables from IOT hospitals, loan           來自IOT醫院之應收款項、向一間舉辦
           to a sponsored hospital and the non-current portion of            權醫院提供的貸款以及其他按攤銷成本
           other financial assets at amortised cost have been                計量的金融資產的非即期部分的公允價
           calculated by discounting the expected future cash                值按照其預期未來現金流量，採用具有
           flows using rates currently available for instruments             類似條款、信貸風險及剩餘到期期限的
           with similar terms, credit risk and remaining maturities.         工具現時可得利率貼現來計算。

           The fair value of the listed financial asset at FVTPL is          以公允價值計量且其變動計入損益的上
           based on quoted market prices.                                    市金融資產的公允價值基於市場報價。

           The fair values of the unlisted financial assets at               以公允價值計量且其變動計入損益的非
           FVTPL are estimated by using a discounted cash flow               上市金融資產的公允價值是根據具有類
           valuation model based on the market interest rates of             似條款和風險的工具的市場利率，採用
           instruments with similar terms and risks.                         折現現金流量估值模型估算的。

           The Group’s principal financial instruments comprise               本集團的主要金融工具包括現金和現金

           interest-bearing bank borrowings. The main purpose of              醫院款項以及計息銀行借款。這些金融
           these financial instruments is to finance the Group’s              工具的主要目的是為本集團的運營提供
           operations. The Group has various other financial                  資金。本集團還有其他各種金融資產和
           assets and liabilities such as receivables from IOT                負債，例如來自IOT醫院之應收款項、
           hospitals, loan to a sponsored hospital, trade and bills           向一間舉辦權醫院提供的貸款、應收貿
           receivables, financial assets included in prepayments,             易款項及應收票據、計入預付款項、保
           deposits and other receivables, balances with related              證金及其他應收款項的金融資產、與關
           parties, trade and bills payables, financial liabilities           聯 方 的 結 餘、應 付 貿 易 款 項 及 應 付 票
           included in other payables and accruals, and lease                 據、計入其他應付款項及應計費用的金
           liabilities, which mainly arise directly from its                  融負債，以及租賃負債，主要直接來自
           operations.                                                        其經營活動。

           The main risks arising from the Group’s financial                  本集團金融工具產生的主要風險是利率
           instruments are interest rate risk, foreign currency risk,         風險、外匯風險、信貸風險、流動資金
           credit risk, liquidity risk and equity price risk. The board       風險及權益價格風險。董事會審查並同
           of directors reviews and agrees policies for managing              意管理每種風險的政策，現概述如下。
           each of these risks and they are summarised below.

           Interest rate risk                                                 利率風險
           The Group’s exposure to the risk of changes in market              本集團承受的市場利率變動風險主要與
           interest rates relates primarily to the Group’s loan to a          本集團向一間舉辦權醫院提供的貸款以
           sponsored hospital and interest-bearing bank                       及具有浮動利率的計息銀行借款有關。
           borrowings with floating interest rates. The Group does            本集團並無使用衍生金融工具對沖其利
           not use derivative financial instruments to hedge its              率風險。本集團通過密切監控利率變動
           interest rate risk. The Group mitigates this risk by               並定期審查其可用信貸額度及其使用情
           closely monitoring the movements in interest rates and             況來減輕這種風險。
           reviewing its available credit facilities and their
           utilisation regularly.

   Liquidity risk                                                               流動資金風險
   Liquidity risk is the risk that the Group will encounter                     流動資金風險指本集團因資金短缺而難
   difficulty in meeting financial obligations due to                           以履行財務責任的風險。本集團面臨的
   shortage of funds. The Group’s exposure to liquidity                         流動資金風險主要來自金融資產和負債
   risk arises primarily from mismatches of the maturities                      的到期日錯配。本集團通過考慮金融負
   of financial assets and liabilities. The Group monitors                      債和金融資產的到期日來監控資金短缺
   its risk to a shortage of funds by considering the                           的風險。
   maturities of both its financial liabilities and financial
   assets.

   The Group’s objective is to maintain a balance                               本集團的目標是通過使用銀行借款在資
   between continuity of funding and flexibility through                        金的連續性和靈活性之間保持平衡。本
   the use of bank borrowings. The Group aims to                                集團旨在維持足夠的現金及現金等價
   maintain sufficient cash and cash equivalents to meet                        物，以滿足其流動資金需求。
   its liquidity requirements.

   The maturity profile of the Group’s financial liabilities as                 根據合同未貼現付款，報告期末本集團
   at the end of the reporting period, based on the                             金融負債的到期情況如下：
   contractual undiscounted payments, is as follows:

           equity securities decrease as a result of changes in the              券價值的變動導致的權益證券公允價值
           levels of equity indices and the value of individual                  下跌的風險。本集團的權益價格風險敞
           securities. The Group is exposed to equity price risk                 口源自於2020年12月31日計入以公允
           arising from an equity investment included in financial               價值計量且其變動計入損益的金融資產
           assets at FVTPL (note 23) as at 31 December 2020.                     的 股 本 投 資（附 註23）。本 集 團 的 上 市
           The Group’s listed investment is listed on the Hong                   投資於香港聯交所上市，且於報告期末
           Kong Stock Exchange and is valued at a quoted market                  以市場報價估值。
           price at the end of the reporting period.

                       hospitals, the legal income of such hospitals shall only be used in                               用）根 據 有 關 醫 院 的 組 織 章 程 細 則 使 用，因
                       line with its purpose and within its business scope, and where                                    此，收入淨額不可分派予舉辦人作為股息，

      「武鋼醫院集團」                     指    武鋼醫院、武漢鋼鐵（集團）公司第二職工醫院及其附屬的多家醫院及社
                                        區診所的統稱

      「徐礦醫院」                       指    徐州市礦山醫院

      「燕化醫院」                       指    北京燕化醫院

      「燕化醫院集團」                     指    燕化醫院及其附屬之社區診所的統稱

      「燕化醫院投資管理協議」                 指    由華潤醫院管理諮詢、燕化醫院以及燕化鳳凰簽訂的日期為2008年2月
                                        4日 的 醫 院 投 資 管 理 協 議，於2008年4月、2010年12月、2011年6月、
                                        2013年7月、2013年9月及2013年10月經補充

      「燕化IOT協議」                    指    由華潤醫院管理諮詢、燕化醫院以及燕化鳳凰簽訂的日期為2008年2月
                                        1日的醫院管理權利與投資框架協議，及燕化醫院投資管理協議的統稱，
                                        於2008年4月、2010年12月、2011年6月、2013年6月、2013年7月、
                                        2013年9月及2013年10月經補充

      「燕化鳳凰」                       指    北京燕化鳳凰醫療資產管理有限公司，一間於2005年7月18日根據中國
                                        法律註冊成立的有限責任公司

      在本報告中，除文義另有所指外，「聯繫人」、「關連人士」、「關連交易」、「附屬公司」及「主要股東」應具有上
      市規則所賦予該等詞彙的相同涵義。
